Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the 3d structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 962. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 517 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to 3d structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 962/517 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
